# Task
You will be given a **Requirement** and a **Class Diagram** in PlantUML. Your task is to annotate every operation listed in **Operations** with a specification.

An operation specification must describe the following sections about the operation:
1. Summary: the summary of functionality;
2. Algorithm: algorithm or pseudo code;
3. Related Features: a list of structural or behavior features within this class that shall be accessed;
4. Related Classes: a list of classes and data types defined in **Class Diagram** that shall be accessed, in the format of `Related Classes: ClsA, CLsB, ...` or `Related Classes: None`;
5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
7. Pre-condition: the condition that the system and the input must satisfy before the operation is executed;
8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

Finish this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Take the principle of modularization into account.
2. For each operation, determine its functional boundary based on the given requirement. Ensure high cohesion and low coupling.
3. If there is any unspecified details, such as undefined constants, rates, and ratios, then you must infer them.
4. Make sure that all requirements can be covered.

# Requirement
```text
We want to automate the management of a small municipal library. The library has a name and manages a collection of documents and a set of registered members. Members have a first name and a surname. Documents are uniquely identified by their title. Every document has a title. A volume is a kind of document and also has an author. A book is a kind of volume and also has a bookId that identifies the physical or catalogued copy. A dictionary is a kind of volume and also has a dictionaryId that identifies the dictionary entry in the library catalog. A comic is also a kind of volume and has a recipient name. A journal is a kind of document and has a publication date. Only books can be borrowed.
A registered member can borrow or return a book, and the system must always be able to list the titles currently borrowed by that member. A member can hold at most three borrowed books at the same time. When a borrowing succeeds, the system creates a borrow record containing the borrowing date, the borrowed book, and a return due date exactly seven days later. A borrowed book can be extended on request when the request is made before the current due date. A return is accepted only when the member currently holds that book and the book is not overdue. The system records dates in the format "yyyy-MM-dd".
Functional requirement:
1. Register a new member. The library registers a new member from a first name and a surname. The first name and surname must not be null, empty, or whitespace-only. The library rejects a registration when another member already has exactly the same full name. A successful registration creates the member with no borrowed books and returns true. Any rejected registration returns false.

2. Borrow a book and create a record in the system. A member borrows a document by title. The library accepts the request only when the title exists, the document is a book, the book is not already borrowed by any member, and the member currently holds fewer than three books. A successful borrowing creates a borrow record with today's date as the borrowing date and a due date exactly seven days later, then returns true. Any rejected borrowing returns false.

3. Extend a borrowed record's return due date. A member may extend the due date of one of their own borrowed books by title. When today is on or after the borrowing date and strictly before the current due date, the library moves the due date forward by seven days and returns the new due date. Otherwise, the due date remains unchanged and the original due date is returned.

4. Return a book. A member returns a borrowed book by title. If the member does not currently hold that title, the operation returns false. If the borrowed book is overdue, the return is rejected, the borrow record remains active, and the operation returns false. Otherwise the borrow record is removed and the operation returns true.

5. List all borrowed book titles for a member. A member can list the titles of all currently borrowed books. If the member has no active borrowed books, the operation returns an empty list.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title municipalLibrary Model

class Book {
    bookId : String
}
class BorrowRecord {
    borrowingDate : Date
    returnDue : Date
    book : Book
    extendDueDate(today : Date) : Date
    isOverdue(today : Date) : boolean
}
class Comic {
    recipientName : String
}
class Dictionary {
    dictionaryId : String
}
abstract class Document {
    title : String
}
class Journal {
    publicationDate : Date
}
class Library {
    name : String
    documents : List<Document>
    members : List<Member>
    addDocument(doc : Document) : void
    registerMember(firstName : String, surname : String) : boolean
}
class Member {
    firstName : String
    surname : String
    borrowRecords : List<BorrowRecord>
    library : Library
    borrowBook(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : boolean
    extendReturnDueDate(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : Date
    listBorrowedBookTitles() : List<String>
    returnBook(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : boolean
}
abstract class Volume {
    author : String
}

Book --|> Volume
BorrowRecord --> Book : book
Comic --|> Volume
Dictionary --|> Volume
Journal --|> Document
Library *-- "*" Document : documents
Library *-- "*" Member : members  'opposite: library
Member *-- "*" BorrowRecord : borrowRecords
Member --> Library : library  'opposite: members
Volume --|> Document
@enduml
```

+ BorrowRecord::isOverdue
+ Library::addDocument
+ Library::registerMember
+ Member::borrowBook
+ Member::extendReturnDueDate
+ Member::listBorrowedBookTitles
+ Member::returnBook

    END IF
  END FOR
  IF targetDoc IS NULL OR NOT targetDoc INSTANCE OF Book THEN
    RETURN FALSE
  END IF
  FOR EACH rec IN borrowRecords DO
    IF rec.book EQUALS targetDoc THEN
      RETURN FALSE
    END IF
  END FOR
  IF borrowRecords.size() >= 3 THEN
    RETURN FALSE
  END IF
  CREATE NEW record
  SET record.borrowingDate = today
  SET record.returnDue = today + 7 days
  SET record.book = targetDoc
  ADD record TO borrowRecords
  RETURN TRUE
Related Features:
  - borrowRecords
  - library
Related Classes: Library, Book, BorrowRecord, Document
Input: bookTitle (String, title of the book to borrow), today (Date, format "yyyy-MM-dd", current date)
Output: boolean (true if borrowing succeeds, false if conditions are not met)
Pre-condition: bookTitle and today are valid; library and borrowRecords references are initialized.
Post-condition: If true, a new BorrowRecord is added with today as borrowingDate and today+7d as returnDue. The book is implicitly reserved. If false, borrowRecords remains unchanged.}
62392 [main] INFO  du.ustb.sei.mde.mwe2.EcoreGenerator  - Verify Annotations

# Task
You will be given a **Requirement**, a **Class Diagram** in PlantUML, and a list of operation specifications. Your task is to correct the specifications that are inconsistent with the requirement.

An operation specification must describe the following sections about the operation:
1. Summary: the summary of functionality;
2. Algorithm: algorithm or pseudo code;
3. Related Features: a list of structural or behavior features within this class that shall be accessed;
4. Related Classes: a list of classes and data types defined in **Class Diagram** that shall be accessed, in the format of `Related Classes: ClsA, CLsB, ...` or `Related Classes: None`;
5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
7. Pre-condition: the condition that the system and the input must satisfy before the operation is executed;
8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

Finish this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Read the requirement and the class diagram.
2. For each operation specification, determine whether it is complete and consistent with the requirement.
3. If there is any error, correct the erroneous specification.
4. Follow the specification format. Output changed specifications only; OMIT unchanged specifications.

# Requirement
```text
We want to automate the management of a small municipal library. The library has a name and manages a collection of documents and a set of registered members. Members have a first name and a surname. Documents are uniquely identified by their title. Every document has a title. A volume is a kind of document and also has an author. A book is a kind of volume and also has a bookId that identifies the physical or catalogued copy. A dictionary is a kind of volume and also has a dictionaryId that identifies the dictionary entry in the library catalog. A comic is also a kind of volume and has a recipient name. A journal is a kind of document and has a publication date. Only books can be borrowed.
A registered member can borrow or return a book, and the system must always be able to list the titles currently borrowed by that member. A member can hold at most three borrowed books at the same time. When a borrowing succeeds, the system creates a borrow record containing the borrowing date, the borrowed book, and a return due date exactly seven days later. A borrowed book can be extended on request when the request is made before the current due date. A return is accepted only when the member currently holds that book and the book is not overdue. The system records dates in the format "yyyy-MM-dd".
Functional requirement:
1. Register a new member. The library registers a new member from a first name and a surname. The first name and surname must not be null, empty, or whitespace-only. The library rejects a registration when another member already has exactly the same full name. A successful registration creates the member with no borrowed books and returns true. Any rejected registration returns false.

2. Borrow a book and create a record in the system. A member borrows a document by title. The library accepts the request only when the title exists, the document is a book, the book is not already borrowed by any member, and the member currently holds fewer than three books. A successful borrowing creates a borrow record with today's date as the borrowing date and a due date exactly seven days later, then returns true. Any rejected borrowing returns false.

3. Extend a borrowed record's return due date. A member may extend the due date of one of their own borrowed books by title. When today is on or after the borrowing date and strictly before the current due date, the library moves the due date forward by seven days and returns the new due date. Otherwise, the due date remains unchanged and the original due date is returned.

4. Return a book. A member returns a borrowed book by title. If the member does not currently hold that title, the operation returns false. If the borrowed book is overdue, the return is rejected, the borrow record remains active, and the operation returns false. Otherwise the borrow record is removed and the operation returns true.

5. List all borrowed book titles for a member. A member can list the titles of all currently borrowed books. If the member has no active borrowed books, the operation returns an empty list.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title municipalLibrary Model

class Book {
    bookId : String
}
class BorrowRecord {
    borrowingDate : Date
    returnDue : Date
    book : Book
    extendDueDate(today : Date) : Date
    isOverdue(today : Date) : boolean
}
class Comic {
    recipientName : String
}
class Dictionary {
    dictionaryId : String
}
abstract class Document {
    title : String
}
class Journal {
    publicationDate : Date
}
class Library {
    name : String
    documents : List<Document>
    members : List<Member>
    addDocument(doc : Document) : void
    registerMember(firstName : String, surname : String) : boolean
}
class Member {
    firstName : String
    surname : String
    borrowRecords : List<BorrowRecord>
    library : Library
    borrowBook(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : boolean
    extendReturnDueDate(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : Date
    listBorrowedBookTitles() : List<String>
    returnBook(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : boolean
}
abstract class Volume {
    author : String
}

Book --|> Volume
BorrowRecord --> Book : book
Comic --|> Volume
Dictionary --|> Volume
Journal --|> Document
Library *-- "*" Document : documents
Library *-- "*" Member : members  'opposite: library
Member *-- "*" BorrowRecord : borrowRecords
Member --> Library : library  'opposite: members
Volume --|> Document
@enduml
```

# Operation Specifications
Library::addDocument
Summary: Inserts a new document into the library's document collection.
Algorithm:
  APPEND doc TO documents
Related Features:
  - documents
Related Classes: Document
Input: doc (Document, an instance of a concrete document subtype such as Book, Journal, Dictionary, or Comic)
Output: void
Pre-condition: doc is not null.
Post-condition: documents list contains the newly added doc. The collection size increases by one.

Library::registerMember
Summary: Validates input parameters and creates a new member record if the name is valid and unique.
Algorithm:
  IF firstName IS NULL OR firstName.trim().isEmpty() OR surname IS NULL OR surname.trim().isEmpty() THEN
    RETURN FALSE
  END IF
  FOR EACH m IN members DO
    IF m.firstName EQUALS firstName AND m.surname EQUALS surname THEN
      RETURN FALSE
    END IF
  END FOR
  CREATE NEW member
  SET member.firstName = firstName
  SET member.surname = surname
  ADD member TO members
  RETURN TRUE
Related Features:
  - members
  - Member.firstName
  - Member.surname
Related Classes: Member
Input: firstName (String, member's first name), surname (String, member's surname)
Output: boolean (true if registration succeeds, false if validation fails or full name already exists)
Pre-condition: firstName and surname are provided strings.
Post-condition: If true, a new Member instance is instantiated and appended to members with the specified names. If false, members list remains unchanged.

# Operation Specifications
BorrowRecord::extendDueDate
Summary: Advances the return due date by seven days if the extension request falls within the valid temporal window; otherwise leaves it unchanged.
Algorithm:
  IF today >= borrowingDate AND today < returnDue THEN
    returnDue = returnDue + 7 days
  END IF
  RETURN returnDue
Related Features:
  - borrowingDate
  - returnDue
Related Classes: None
Input: today (Date, format "yyyy-MM-dd", represents the current system date when the extension is requested)
Output: Date (format "yyyy-MM-dd", the updated due date if extended, otherwise the original due date)
Pre-condition: today is a valid date; borrowingDate and returnDue are properly initialized.
Post-condition: If the temporal condition is satisfied, returnDue is exactly seven days later than its previous value. The returned value matches the current state of returnDue. No other attributes are modified.

BorrowRecord::isOverdue
Summary: Determines whether the current date has passed the scheduled return due date.
Algorithm:
  RETURN today > returnDue
Related Features:
  - returnDue
Related Classes: None
Input: today (Date, format "yyyy-MM-dd", represents the current system date)
Output: boolean (true if today is strictly after returnDue, false otherwise)
Pre-condition: today is a valid date; returnDue is initialized.
Post-condition: Returns true if and only if today is chronologically later than returnDue. The system state remains completely unchanged.

```plantuml
Comic --|> Volume
Dictionary --|> Volume
Journal --|> Document
Library *-- "*" Document : documents
Library *-- "*" Member : members  'opposite: library
Member *-- "*" BorrowRecord : borrowRecords
Member --> Library : library  'opposite: members
Volume --|> Document
@enduml
```

# Operation Specifications
BorrowRecord::extendDueDate
Summary: Advances the return due date by seven days if the extension request falls within the valid temporal window; otherwise leaves it unchanged.
Algorithm:
  IF today >= borrowingDate AND today < returnDue THEN
    returnDue = returnDue + 7 days
  END IF
  RETURN returnDue
Related Features:
  - borrowingDate
  - returnDue
Related Classes: None
Input: today (Date, format "yyyy-MM-dd", represents the current system date when the extension is requested)
Output: Date (format "yyyy-MM-dd", the updated due date if extended, otherwise the original due date)
Pre-condition: today is a valid date; borrowingDate and returnDue are properly initialized.
Post-condition: If the temporal condition is satisfied, returnDue is exactly seven days later than its previous value. The returned value matches the current state of returnDue. No other attributes are modified.

BorrowRecord::isOverdue
Summary: Determines whether the current date has passed the scheduled return due date.
Algorithm:
  RETURN today > returnDue
Related Features:
  - returnDue
Related Classes: None
Input: today (Date, format "yyyy-MM-dd", represents the current system date)
Output: boolean (true if today is strictly after returnDue, false otherwise)
Pre-condition: today is a valid date; returnDue is initialized.
Post-condition: Returns true if and only if today is chronologically later than returnDue. The system state remains completely unchanged.

You will be given a **Requirement**, a **Class Diagram** in PlantUML, and a list of operation specifications. Your task is to correct the specifications that are inconsistent with the requirement.

# Requirement
```text
We want to automate the management of a small municipal library. The library has a name and manages a collection of documents and a set of registered members. Members have a first name and a surname. Documents are uniquely identified by their title. Every document has a title. A volume is a kind of document and also has an author. A book is a kind of volume and also has a bookId that identifies the physical or catalogued copy. A dictionary is a kind of volume and also has a dictionaryId that identifies the dictionary entry in the library catalog. A comic is also a kind of volume and has a recipient name. A journal is a kind of document and has a publication date. Only books can be borrowed.
A registered member can borrow or return a book, and the system must always be able to list the titles currently borrowed by that member. A member can hold at most three borrowed books at the same time. When a borrowing succeeds, the system creates a borrow record containing the borrowing date, the borrowed book, and a return due date exactly seven days later. A borrowed book can be extended on request when the request is made before the current due date. A return is accepted only when the member currently holds that book and the book is not overdue. The system records dates in the format "yyyy-MM-dd".
Functional requirement:
1. Register a new member. The library registers a new member from a first name and a surname. The first name and surname must not be null, empty, or whitespace-only. The library rejects a registration when another member already has exactly the same full name. A successful registration creates the member with no borrowed books and returns true. Any rejected registration returns false.

2. Borrow a book and create a record in the system. A member borrows a document by title. The library accepts the request only when the title exists, the document is a book, the book is not already borrowed by any member, and the member currently holds fewer than three books. A successful borrowing creates a borrow record with today's date as the borrowing date and a due date exactly seven days later, then returns true. Any rejected borrowing returns false.

3. Extend a borrowed record's return due date. A member may extend the due date of one of their own borrowed books by title. When today is on or after the borrowing date and strictly before the current due date, the library moves the due date forward by seven days and returns the new due date. Otherwise, the due date remains unchanged and the original due date is returned.

4. Return a book. A member returns a borrowed book by title. If the member does not currently hold that title, the operation returns false. If the borrowed book is overdue, the return is rejected, the borrow record remains active, and the operation returns false. Otherwise the borrow record is removed and the operation returns true.

5. List all borrowed book titles for a member. A member can list the titles of all currently borrowed books. If the member has no active borrowed books, the operation returns an empty list.
```

# Operation Specifications
Member::extendReturnDueDate
Summary: Locates the borrow record for a specific book and delegates the date extension logic to it.
Algorithm:
  record = NULL
  FOR EACH rec IN borrowRecords DO
    IF rec.book.title EQUALS bookTitle THEN
      record = rec
      BREAK
    END IF
  END FOR
  IF record IS NOT NULL THEN
    RETURN record.extendDueDate(today)
  ELSE
    RETURN NULL (or throw NotFoundException per implementation choice)
  END IF
Related Features:
  - borrowRecords
Related Classes: BorrowRecord
Input: bookTitle (String, title of the borrowed book to extend), today (Date, format "yyyy-MM-dd", current date)
Output: Date (format "yyyy-MM-dd", the effective due date after potential extension)
Pre-condition: bookTitle and today are valid; borrowRecords is initialized.
Post-condition: If the record is found and meets the temporal constraints, its due date is advanced by 7 days. Returned value matches the record's final state.

Member::listBorrowedBookTitles
Summary: Retrieves the titles of all books currently associated with active borrow records.
Algorithm:
  titles = NEW LIST<STRING>
  FOR EACH rec IN borrowRecords DO
    ADD rec.book.title TO titles
  END FOR
  RETURN titles
Related Features:
  - borrowRecords
Related Classes: Book, Document
Input: None
Output: List<String> (collection of book titles currently borrowed; empty list if no active records)
Pre-condition: borrowRecords list is initialized.
Post-condition: Returns a list containing exactly the titles of books linked to entries in borrowRecords. The list order is not specified. No state is modified.

Member::returnBook
Summary: Processes the return of a book by validating ownership and overdue status before removing the record.
Algorithm:
  record = NULL
  FOR EACH rec IN borrowRecords DO
    IF rec.book.title EQUALS bookTitle THEN
      record = rec
      BREAK
    END IF
  END FOR
  IF record IS NULL THEN
    RETURN FALSE
  END IF
  IF record.isOverdue(today) THEN
    RETURN FALSE
  END IF
  REMOVE record FROM borrowRecords
  RETURN TRUE
Related Features:
  - borrowRecords
Related Classes: BorrowRecord
Input: bookTitle (String, title of the book to return), today (Date, format "yyyy-MM-dd", current date)
Output: boolean (true if return is processed successfully, false otherwise)
Pre-condition: bookTitle and today are valid; borrowRecords is initialized.
Post-condition: If true, the matching borrow record is permanently removed from borrowRecords. If false, borrowRecords remains unchanged and the record stays active.

Member::borrowBook
Summary: Attempts to borrow a book by title, creating a borrow record upon success.
Algorithm:
  targetDoc = NULL
  FOR EACH doc IN library.documents DO
    IF doc.title EQUALS bookTitle THEN
      targetDoc = doc
      BREAK
    END IF
  END FOR
  IF targetDoc IS NULL OR NOT targetDoc INSTANCE OF Book THEN
    RETURN FALSE
  END IF
  FOR EACH rec IN borrowRecords DO
    IF rec.book EQUALS targetDoc THEN
      RETURN FALSE
    END IF
  END FOR
  IF borrowRecords.size() >= 3 THEN
    RETURN FALSE
  END IF
  CREATE NEW record
  SET record.borrowingDate = today
  SET record.returnDue = today + 7 days
  SET record.book = targetDoc
  ADD record TO borrowRecords
  RETURN TRUE
Related Features:
  - borrowRecords
  - library
Related Classes: Library, Book, BorrowRecord, Document
Input: bookTitle (String, title of the book to borrow), today (Date, format "yyyy-MM-dd", current date)
Output: boolean (true if borrowing succeeds, false if conditions are not met)
Pre-condition: bookTitle and today are valid; library and borrowRecords references are initialized.
Post-condition: If true, a new BorrowRecord is added with today as borrowingDate and today+7d as returnDue. The book is implicitly reserved. If false, borrowRecords remains unchanged.

You will be given a **Java Class** generated from an Ecore model by EMF code generator with `suppressInterfaces=true`. Your task is to implement the Java methods unimplemented by taking additional contextual information into account.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information

  1.2 Locate these files in your project.
  1.3 Generate the class summarization.
2. A method is unimplemented if its body contains `// TODO: Please complete this Java method based on the docstring`.
3. For every unimplemented method, 
  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;
  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;
  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the context.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

# Java Class
```java
/**
 * generated by Enhanced EcoreGen
 */
package edu.municipalLibrary;

import java.util.Collection;

import org.eclipse.emf.common.notify.Notification;
import org.eclipse.emf.common.notify.NotificationChain;

import org.eclipse.emf.common.util.EList;

import org.eclipse.emf.ecore.EClass;
```

   * Post-condition: If true, a new BorrowRecord is added with today as borrowingDate and today+7d as returnDue. The book is reserved system-wide and cannot be borrowed by others until returned. If false, borrowRecords remains unchanged.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model todayDataType="edu.municipalLibrary.Date"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean borrowBook(final String bookTitle, final Date today)
  {
    Library library = getLibrary();
    Document targetDoc = null;
    for (Document doc : library.getDocuments()) {
      if (doc.getTitle() != null && doc.getTitle().equals(bookTitle)) {
        targetDoc = doc;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (targetDoc == null || !(targetDoc instanceof Book)) {
      return false;
    }
    for (Member mem : library.getMembers()) {
      for (BorrowRecord rec : mem.getBorrowRecords()) {
        if (rec.getBook() == targetDoc) {
          return false;
        }
      }
    }
    if (getBorrowRecords().size() >= 3) {
      return false;
    }
    BorrowRecord record = MunicipalLibraryFactory.eINSTANCE.createBorrowRecord();
    record.setBorrowingDate(today);
    Calendar cal = Calendar.getInstance();
    cal.setTime(today);
    cal.add(Calendar.DATE, 7);
    record.setReturnDue(cal.getTime());
    record.setBook((Book) targetDoc);
    getBorrowRecords().add(record);
    return true;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Processes the return of a book by validating ownership and overdue status before removing the record.
   * Algorithm:

   * Post-condition: If true, the matching borrow record is permanently removed from borrowRecords. If false, borrowRecords remains unchanged and the record stays active.

   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"

  public boolean returnBook(final String bookTitle, final Date today)
  {
    BorrowRecord record = null;
    for (BorrowRecord rec : getBorrowRecords()) {
      if (rec.getBook() != null && rec.getBook().getTitle() != null && rec.getBook().getTitle().equals(bookTitle)) {
        record = rec;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (record == null) {
      return false;
    }
    if (record.isOverdue(today)) {
      return false;
    }
    getBorrowRecords().remove(record);
    return true;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Locates the borrow record for a specific book and extends its return due date if valid.
   * Algorithm:
   *   record = NULL
   *   FOR EACH rec IN borrowRecords DO
   *     IF rec.book.title EQUALS bookTitle THEN
   *       record = rec
   *       BREAK
   *     END IF
   *   END FOR
   *   IF record IS NULL THEN
   *     RETURN NULL (or handle missing record per implementation)
   *   END IF
   *   IF today >= record.borrowingDate AND today < record.returnDue THEN
   *     SET record.returnDue = today + 7 days
   *     RETURN record.returnDue
   *   ELSE
   *     RETURN record.returnDue
   *   END IF
   * Related Features:
   *   - borrowRecords
   * Related Classes: BorrowRecord
   * Input: bookTitle (String, title of the borrowed book to extend), today (Date, format "yyyy-MM-dd", current date)
   * Output: Date (format "yyyy-MM-dd", the effective due date after potential extension)
   * Pre-condition: bookTitle and today are valid; borrowRecords is initialized.
   * Post-condition: If the record is found and today is on or after the borrowing date but strictly before the due date, the due date is advanced by 7 days and the new date is returned. Otherwise, the due date remains unchanged and the original due date is returned.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model dataType="edu.municipalLibrary.Date" todayDataType="edu.municipalLibrary.Date"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public Date extendReturnDueDate(final String bookTitle, final Date today)
  {
    BorrowRecord record = null;
    for (BorrowRecord rec : getBorrowRecords()) {
      if (rec.getBook() != null && rec.getBook().getTitle() != null && rec.getBook().getTitle().equals(bookTitle)) {
        record = rec;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (record == null) {
      return null;
    }
    if (today.compareTo(record.getBorrowingDate()) >= 0 && today.compareTo(record.getReturnDue()) < 0) {
      Calendar cal = Calendar.getInstance();
      cal.setTime(today);
      cal.add(Calendar.DATE, 7);
      record.setReturnDue(cal.getTime());
      return record.getReturnDue();
    } else {
      return record.getReturnDue();
    }
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Retrieves the titles of all books currently associated with active borrow records.
   * Algorithm:
   *   titles = NEW LIST<STRING>
   *   FOR EACH rec IN borrowRecords DO
   *     ADD rec.book.title TO titles
   *   END FOR
   *   RETURN titles
   * Related Features:
   *   - borrowRecords
   * Related Classes: Book, Document
   * Input: None
   * Output: List<String> (collection of book titles currently borrowed; empty list if no active records)
   * Pre-condition: borrowRecords list is initialized.
   * Post-condition: Returns a list containing exactly the titles of books linked to entries in borrowRecords. The list order is not specified. No state is modified.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public EList<String> listBorrowedBookTitles()
  {
    EList<String> titles = new BasicEList<>();
    for (BorrowRecord rec : getBorrowRecords()) {
      if (rec.getBook() != null && rec.getBook().getTitle() != null) {
        titles.add(rec.getBook().getTitle());
      }
    }
    return titles;
  }

# Task
You will be given a **Requirement** and a **Class Diagram** in PlantUML. Your task is to annotate every operation listed in **Operations** with a specification.

An operation specification must describe the following sections about the operation:
1. Summary: the summary of functionality;
2. Algorithm: algorithm or pseudo code;
3. Related Features: a list of structural or behavior features within this class that shall be accessed;
4. Related Classes: a list of classes and data types defined in **Class Diagram** that shall be accessed, in the format of `Related Classes: ClsA, CLsB, ...` or `Related Classes: None`;
5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
7. Pre-condition: the condition that the system and the input must satisfy before the operation is executed;
8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

Finish this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Take the principle of modularization into account.
2. For each operation, determine its functional boundary based on the given requirement. Ensure high cohesion and low coupling.
3. If there is any unspecified details, such as undefined constants, rates, and ratios, then you must infer them.
4. Make sure that all requirements can be covered.

# Requirement
```text
We want to automate the management of a small municipal library. The library has a name and manages a collection of documents and a set of registered members. Members have a first name and a surname. Documents are uniquely identified by their title. Every document has a title. A volume is a kind of document and also has an author. A book is a kind of volume and also has a bookId that identifies the physical or catalogued copy. A dictionary is a kind of volume and also has a dictionaryId that identifies the dictionary entry in the library catalog. A comic is also a kind of volume and has a recipient name. A journal is a kind of document and has a publication date. Only books can be borrowed.
A registered member can borrow or return a book, and the system must always be able to list the titles currently borrowed by that member. A member can hold at most three borrowed books at the same time. When a borrowing succeeds, the system creates a borrow record containing the borrowing date, the borrowed book, and a return due date exactly seven days later. A borrowed book can be extended on request when the request is made before the current due date. A return is accepted only when the member currently holds that book and the book is not overdue. The system records dates in the format "yyyy-MM-dd".
Functional requirement:
1. Register a new member. The library registers a new member from a first name and a surname. The first name and surname must not be null, empty, or whitespace-only. The library rejects a registration when another member already has exactly the same full name. A successful registration creates the member with no borrowed books and returns true. Any rejected registration returns false.

2. Borrow a book and create a record in the system. A member borrows a document by title. The library accepts the request only when the title exists, the document is a book, the book is not already borrowed by any member, and the member currently holds fewer than three books. A successful borrowing creates a borrow record with today's date as the borrowing date and a due date exactly seven days later, then returns true. Any rejected borrowing returns false.

3. Extend a borrowed record's return due date. A member may extend the due date of one of their own borrowed books by title. When today is on or after the borrowing date and strictly before the current due date, the library moves the due date forward by seven days and returns the new due date. Otherwise, the due date remains unchanged and the original due date is returned.

4. Return a book. A member returns a borrowed book by title. If the member does not currently hold that title, the operation returns false. If the borrowed book is overdue, the return is rejected, the borrow record remains active, and the operation returns false. Otherwise the borrow record is removed and the operation returns true.

5. List all borrowed book titles for a member. A member can list the titles of all currently borrowed books. If the member has no active borrowed books, the operation returns an empty list.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title municipalLibrary Model

class Book {
    bookId : String
}
class BorrowRecord {
    borrowingDate : Date
    returnDue : Date
    book : Book
    extendDueDate(today : Date) : Date
    isOverdue(today : Date) : boolean
}
class Comic {
    recipientName : String
}
class Dictionary {
    dictionaryId : String
}
abstract class Document {
    title : String
}
class Journal {
    publicationDate : Date
}
class Library {
    name : String
    documents : List<Document>
    members : List<Member>
    addDocument(doc : Document) : void
    registerMember(firstName : String, surname : String) : boolean
}
class Member {
    firstName : String
    surname : String
    borrowRecords : List<BorrowRecord>
    library : Library
    borrowBook(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : boolean
    extendReturnDueDate(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : Date
    listBorrowedBookTitles() : List<String>
    returnBook(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : boolean
}
abstract class Volume {
    author : String
}

Book --|> Volume
BorrowRecord --> Book : book
Comic --|> Volume
Dictionary --|> Volume
Journal --|> Document
Library *-- "*" Document : documents
Library *-- "*" Member : members  'opposite: library
Member *-- "*" BorrowRecord : borrowRecords
Member --> Library : library  'opposite: members
Volume --|> Document
@enduml
```

+ BorrowRecord::isOverdue

+ Library::registerMember
+ Member::borrowBook
+ Member::extendReturnDueDate
+ Member::listBorrowedBookTitles
+ Member::returnBook

   IF firstName IS NULL OR EMPTY OR WHITESPACE_ONLY OR surname IS NULL OR EMPTY OR WHITESPACE_ONLY THEN RETURN FALSE
   fullName = firstName + " " + surname
   FOR EACH m IN members DO
       IF m.firstName + " " + m.surname EQUALS fullName THEN RETURN FALSE

   newMember.library = THIS

# Task
You will be given a **Requirement**, a **Class Diagram** in PlantUML, and a list of operation specifications. Your task is to correct the specifications that are inconsistent with the requirement.

An operation specification must describe the following sections about the operation:
1. Summary: the summary of functionality;
2. Algorithm: algorithm or pseudo code;
3. Related Features: a list of structural or behavior features within this class that shall be accessed;
4. Related Classes: a list of classes and data types defined in **Class Diagram** that shall be accessed, in the format of `Related Classes: ClsA, CLsB, ...` or `Related Classes: None`;
5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
7. Pre-condition: the condition that the system and the input must satisfy before the operation is executed;
8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

Finish this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Read the requirement and the class diagram.
2. For each operation specification, determine whether it is complete and consistent with the requirement.
3. If there is any error, correct the erroneous specification.
4. Follow the specification format. Output changed specifications only; OMIT unchanged specifications.

# Requirement
```text
We want to automate the management of a small municipal library. The library has a name and manages a collection of documents and a set of registered members. Members have a first name and a surname. Documents are uniquely identified by their title. Every document has a title. A volume is a kind of document and also has an author. A book is a kind of volume and also has a bookId that identifies the physical or catalogued copy. A dictionary is a kind of volume and also has a dictionaryId that identifies the dictionary entry in the library catalog. A comic is also a kind of volume and has a recipient name. A journal is a kind of document and has a publication date. Only books can be borrowed.
A registered member can borrow or return a book, and the system must always be able to list the titles currently borrowed by that member. A member can hold at most three borrowed books at the same time. When a borrowing succeeds, the system creates a borrow record containing the borrowing date, the borrowed book, and a return due date exactly seven days later. A borrowed book can be extended on request when the request is made before the current due date. A return is accepted only when the member currently holds that book and the book is not overdue. The system records dates in the format "yyyy-MM-dd".
Functional requirement:
1. Register a new member. The library registers a new member from a first name and a surname. The first name and surname must not be null, empty, or whitespace-only. The library rejects a registration when another member already has exactly the same full name. A successful registration creates the member with no borrowed books and returns true. Any rejected registration returns false.

2. Borrow a book and create a record in the system. A member borrows a document by title. The library accepts the request only when the title exists, the document is a book, the book is not already borrowed by any member, and the member currently holds fewer than three books. A successful borrowing creates a borrow record with today's date as the borrowing date and a due date exactly seven days later, then returns true. Any rejected borrowing returns false.

3. Extend a borrowed record's return due date. A member may extend the due date of one of their own borrowed books by title. When today is on or after the borrowing date and strictly before the current due date, the library moves the due date forward by seven days and returns the new due date. Otherwise, the due date remains unchanged and the original due date is returned.

4. Return a book. A member returns a borrowed book by title. If the member does not currently hold that title, the operation returns false. If the borrowed book is overdue, the return is rejected, the borrow record remains active, and the operation returns false. Otherwise the borrow record is removed and the operation returns true.

5. List all borrowed book titles for a member. A member can list the titles of all currently borrowed books. If the member has no active borrowed books, the operation returns an empty list.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title municipalLibrary Model

class Book {
    bookId : String
}
class BorrowRecord {
    borrowingDate : Date
    returnDue : Date
    book : Book
    extendDueDate(today : Date) : Date
    isOverdue(today : Date) : boolean
}
class Comic {
    recipientName : String
}
class Dictionary {
    dictionaryId : String
}
abstract class Document {
    title : String
}
class Journal {
    publicationDate : Date
}
class Library {
    name : String
    documents : List<Document>
    members : List<Member>
    addDocument(doc : Document) : void
    registerMember(firstName : String, surname : String) : boolean
}
class Member {
    firstName : String
    surname : String
    borrowRecords : List<BorrowRecord>
    library : Library
    borrowBook(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : boolean
    extendReturnDueDate(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : Date
    listBorrowedBookTitles() : List<String>
    returnBook(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : boolean
}
abstract class Volume {
    author : String
}

Book --|> Volume
BorrowRecord --> Book : book
Comic --|> Volume
Dictionary --|> Volume
Journal --|> Document
Library *-- "*" Document : documents
Library *-- "*" Member : members  'opposite: library
Member *-- "*" BorrowRecord : borrowRecords
Member --> Library : library  'opposite: members
Volume --|> Document
@enduml
```

# Operation Specifications
Member::listBorrowedBookTitles
1. Summary: Retrieves a collection of titles for all books currently borrowed by the member.
2. Algorithm: 
   titles = NEW List<String>()
   FOR EACH record IN borrowRecords DO
       titles.add(record.book.title)
   END FOR
   RETURN titles
3. Related Features: borrowRecords
4. Related Classes: BorrowRecord, Book, Document
5. Input: None.
6. Output: List<String>: A list containing the titles of all actively borrowed books. Returns an empty list if borrowRecords is empty.
7. Pre-condition: Member instance exists.
8. Post-condition: Returns a snapshot of current borrowed titles. No system state or borrowRecords are modified.

Member::extendReturnDueDate
1. Summary: Requests an extension for the return due date of a specific borrowed book.
2. Algorithm: 
   targetRecord = FIND record IN borrowRecords WHERE record.book.title EQUALS bookTitle
   IF targetRecord IS NULL THEN RETURN null (or handle as per error policy)
   RETURN targetRecord.extendDueDate(today)
3. Related Features: borrowRecords
4. Related Classes: BorrowRecord, Book, Document
5. Input: bookTitle (String): Title of the borrowed book whose due date is to be extended. today (Date): Current date. Format: "yyyy-MM-dd".
6. Output: Date: The resulting due date after evaluation. Format: "yyyy-MM-dd".
7. Pre-condition: The member currently holds an active borrow record matching the provided bookTitle.
8. Post-condition: Delegates temporal validation and date adjustment to BorrowRecord::extendDueDate. If the request window is valid, due date advances by 7 days; otherwise, it remains unchanged. Returns the final effective due date.

Member::borrowBook
1. Summary: Allows the member to borrow a book identified by its title, enforcing availability and capacity constraints.
2. Algorithm: 
   targetDoc = FIND doc IN library.documents WHERE doc.title EQUALS bookTitle
   IF targetDoc IS NULL OR NOT (targetDoc INSTANCEOF Book) THEN RETURN FALSE
   IF EXISTS ANY record IN ALL members.borrowRecords WHERE record.book EQUALS targetDoc THEN RETURN FALSE
   IF borrowRecords.size() >= 3 THEN RETURN FALSE
   newRecord = NEW BorrowRecord(borrowingDate: today, returnDue: today + 7 days, book: targetDoc)
   borrowRecords.add(newRecord)
   RETURN TRUE
3. Related Features: borrowRecords, library
4. Related Classes: Library, Book, Document, BorrowRecord
5. Input: bookTitle (String): The exact title of the book to borrow. today (Date): The current date of borrowing. Format: "yyyy-MM-dd".
6. Output: boolean: True if the borrowing operation succeeds, false otherwise.
7. Pre-condition: Member instance exists. Target document exists in the library catalog.
8. Post-condition: If true, a new BorrowRecord is added to borrowRecords with correct dates, and the book becomes unavailable to others. If false, borrowRecords and library state remain unchanged.

Member::returnBook
1. Summary: Processes the return of a borrowed book, verifying ownership and compliance with overdue restrictions.
2. Algorithm: 
   targetRecord = FIND record IN borrowRecords WHERE record.book.title EQUALS bookTitle
   IF targetRecord IS NULL THEN RETURN FALSE
   IF targetRecord.isOverdue(today) THEN RETURN FALSE
   borrowRecords.remove(targetRecord)
   RETURN TRUE
3. Related Features: borrowRecords
4. Related Classes: BorrowRecord, Book, Document
5. Input: bookTitle (String): Title of the book being returned. today (Date): Current date. Format: "yyyy-MM-dd".
6. Output: boolean: True if the return is accepted and the record is cleared, false otherwise.
7. Pre-condition: Member instance exists and has previously borrowed the specified book.
8. Post-condition: If true, the corresponding BorrowRecord is removed from borrowRecords, freeing the book for future loans. If false (book not held or overdue), borrowRecords remains completely unchanged.

# Task
You will be given a **Requirement**, a **Class Diagram** in PlantUML, and a list of operation specifications. Your task is to correct the specifications that are inconsistent with the requirement.

An operation specification must describe the following sections about the operation:
1. Summary: the summary of functionality;
2. Algorithm: algorithm or pseudo code;
3. Related Features: a list of structural or behavior features within this class that shall be accessed;
4. Related Classes: a list of classes and data types defined in **Class Diagram** that shall be accessed, in the format of `Related Classes: ClsA, CLsB, ...` or `Related Classes: None`;
5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
7. Pre-condition: the condition that the system and the input must satisfy before the operation is executed;
8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

Finish this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Read the requirement and the class diagram.
2. For each operation specification, determine whether it is complete and consistent with the requirement.
3. If there is any error, correct the erroneous specification.
4. Follow the specification format. Output changed specifications only; OMIT unchanged specifications.

# Requirement
```text
We want to automate the management of a small municipal library. The library has a name and manages a collection of documents and a set of registered members. Members have a first name and a surname. Documents are uniquely identified by their title. Every document has a title. A volume is a kind of document and also has an author. A book is a kind of volume and also has a bookId that identifies the physical or catalogued copy. A dictionary is a kind of volume and also has a dictionaryId that identifies the dictionary entry in the library catalog. A comic is also a kind of volume and has a recipient name. A journal is a kind of document and has a publication date. Only books can be borrowed.
A registered member can borrow or return a book, and the system must always be able to list the titles currently borrowed by that member. A member can hold at most three borrowed books at the same time. When a borrowing succeeds, the system creates a borrow record containing the borrowing date, the borrowed book, and a return due date exactly seven days later. A borrowed book can be extended on request when the request is made before the current due date. A return is accepted only when the member currently holds that book and the book is not overdue. The system records dates in the format "yyyy-MM-dd".
Functional requirement:
1. Register a new member. The library registers a new member from a first name and a surname. The first name and surname must not be null, empty, or whitespace-only. The library rejects a registration when another member already has exactly the same full name. A successful registration creates the member with no borrowed books and returns true. Any rejected registration returns false.

2. Borrow a book and create a record in the system. A member borrows a document by title. The library accepts the request only when the title exists, the document is a book, the book is not already borrowed by any member, and the member currently holds fewer than three books. A successful borrowing creates a borrow record with today's date as the borrowing date and a due date exactly seven days later, then returns true. Any rejected borrowing returns false.

3. Extend a borrowed record's return due date. A member may extend the due date of one of their own borrowed books by title. When today is on or after the borrowing date and strictly before the current due date, the library moves the due date forward by seven days and returns the new due date. Otherwise, the due date remains unchanged and the original due date is returned.

4. Return a book. A member returns a borrowed book by title. If the member does not currently hold that title, the operation returns false. If the borrowed book is overdue, the return is rejected, the borrow record remains active, and the operation returns false. Otherwise the borrow record is removed and the operation returns true.

5. List all borrowed book titles for a member. A member can list the titles of all currently borrowed books. If the member has no active borrowed books, the operation returns an empty list.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title municipalLibrary Model

class Book {
    bookId : String
}
class BorrowRecord {
    borrowingDate : Date
    returnDue : Date
    book : Book
    extendDueDate(today : Date) : Date
    isOverdue(today : Date) : boolean
}
class Comic {
    recipientName : String
}
class Dictionary {
    dictionaryId : String
}
abstract class Document {
    title : String
}
class Journal {
    publicationDate : Date
}
class Library {
    name : String
    documents : List<Document>
    members : List<Member>
    addDocument(doc : Document) : void
    registerMember(firstName : String, surname : String) : boolean
}
class Member {
    firstName : String
    surname : String
    borrowRecords : List<BorrowRecord>
    library : Library
    borrowBook(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : boolean
    extendReturnDueDate(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : Date
    listBorrowedBookTitles() : List<String>
    returnBook(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : boolean
}
abstract class Volume {
    author : String
}

Book --|> Volume
BorrowRecord --> Book : book
Comic --|> Volume
Dictionary --|> Volume
Journal --|> Document
Library *-- "*" Document : documents
Library *-- "*" Member : members  'opposite: library
Member *-- "*" BorrowRecord : borrowRecords
Member --> Library : library  'opposite: members
Volume --|> Document
@enduml
```

# Operation Specifications
Library::registerMember
1. Summary: Registers a new member in the library system after validating inputs and checking for naming conflicts.
2. Algorithm: 
   IF firstName IS NULL OR EMPTY OR WHITESPACE_ONLY OR surname IS NULL OR EMPTY OR WHITESPACE_ONLY THEN RETURN FALSE
   fullName = firstName + " " + surname
   FOR EACH m IN members DO
       IF m.firstName + " " + m.surname EQUALS fullName THEN RETURN FALSE
   END FOR
   newMember = NEW Member(firstName, surname)
   newMember.library = THIS
   members.add(newMember)
   RETURN TRUE
3. Related Features: members
4. Related Classes: Member
5. Input: firstName (String), surname (String). Both must not be null, empty, or whitespace-only.
6. Output: boolean: True if registration succeeds, false if validation fails or a duplicate full name exists.
7. Pre-condition: Library instance exists and is ready to accept new members.
8. Post-condition: If true, a new Member with an empty borrowRecords list is created and added to members. If false, the library's member list remains unchanged.

Library::addDocument
1. Summary: Registers a new document into the library's central collection.
2. Algorithm: documents.add(doc)
3. Related Features: documents
4. Related Classes: Document
5. Input: doc (Document): The document instance to be added to the library's inventory.
6. Output: void
7. Pre-condition: doc is not null and represents a valid document type.
8. Post-condition: doc is appended to the documents list. The library's catalog size increases by one.

```result
 */

/*
 * revised description for this operation_2
 */

...
```

# Operation Specifications
BorrowRecord::extendDueDate
1. Summary: Extends the return due date of the borrow record by seven days if the extension request falls within the valid temporal window.
2. Algorithm: 
   IF today >= borrowingDate AND today < returnDue THEN
       returnDue = returnDue + 7 days
   ENDIF
   RETURN returnDue
3. Related Features: borrowingDate, returnDue
4. Related Classes: None
5. Input: today (Date): The date when the extension is requested. Format: "yyyy-MM-dd".
6. Output: Date: The updated due date if conditions are met, otherwise the original due date. Format: "yyyy-MM-dd".
7. Pre-condition: The borrow record is active and has not been returned or cancelled.
8. Post-condition: If valid, returnDue is advanced by exactly 7 days. The method returns the final due date. If invalid, returnDue remains unchanged and the original date is returned.

BorrowRecord::isOverdue
1. Summary: Determines whether the borrow record has exceeded its allowed borrowing period.
2. Algorithm: RETURN today > returnDue
3. Related Features: returnDue
4. Related Classes: None
5. Input: today (Date): The current date to evaluate against the due date. Format: "yyyy-MM-dd".
6. Output: boolean: True if today is strictly after returnDue, false otherwise.
7. Pre-condition: The borrow record exists and is active.
8. Post-condition: No state modifications occur. The method returns a boolean reflecting the overdue status.

```plantuml
BorrowRecord --> Book : book
Comic --|> Volume
Dictionary --|> Volume
Journal --|> Document
Library *-- "*" Document : documents
Library *-- "*" Member : members  'opposite: library
Member *-- "*" BorrowRecord : borrowRecords
Member --> Library : library  'opposite: members
Volume --|> Document
@enduml
```

# Operation Specifications
BorrowRecord::extendDueDate
1. Summary: Extends the return due date of the borrow record by seven days if the extension request falls within the valid temporal window.
2. Algorithm: 
   IF today >= borrowingDate AND today < returnDue THEN
       returnDue = returnDue + 7 days
   ENDIF
   RETURN returnDue
3. Related Features: borrowingDate, returnDue
4. Related Classes: None
5. Input: today (Date): The date when the extension is requested. Format: "yyyy-MM-dd".
6. Output: Date: The updated due date if conditions are met, otherwise the original due date. Format: "yyyy-MM-dd".
7. Pre-condition: The borrow record is active and has not been returned or cancelled.
8. Post-condition: If valid, returnDue is advanced by exactly 7 days. The method returns the final due date. If invalid, returnDue remains unchanged and the original date is returned.

BorrowRecord::isOverdue
1. Summary: Determines whether the borrow record has exceeded its allowed borrowing period.
2. Algorithm: RETURN today > returnDue
3. Related Features: returnDue
4. Related Classes: None
5. Input: today (Date): The current date to evaluate against the due date. Format: "yyyy-MM-dd".
6. Output: boolean: True if today is strictly after returnDue, false otherwise.
7. Pre-condition: The borrow record exists and is active.
8. Post-condition: No state modifications occur. The method returns a boolean reflecting the overdue status.

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * 1. Summary: Allows the member to borrow a book identified by its title, enforcing availability and capacity constraints.
   * 2. Algorithm: 
   *    targetDoc = FIND doc IN library.documents WHERE doc.title EQUALS bookTitle
   *    IF targetDoc IS NULL OR NOT (targetDoc INSTANCEOF Book) THEN RETURN FALSE
   *    IF EXISTS ANY record IN library.members[*].borrowRecords WHERE record.book EQUALS targetDoc THEN RETURN FALSE
   *    IF borrowRecords.size() >= 3 THEN RETURN FALSE
   *    newRecord = NEW BorrowRecord(borrowingDate: today, returnDue: today + 7 days, book: targetDoc)
   *    borrowRecords.add(newRecord)
   *    RETURN TRUE
   * 3. Related Features: borrowRecords, library
   * 4. Related Classes: Library, Book, Document, BorrowRecord
   * 5. Input: bookTitle (String): The exact title of the book to borrow. today (Date): The current date of borrowing. Format: "yyyy-MM-dd".
   * 6. Output: boolean: True if the borrowing operation succeeds, false otherwise.
   * 7. Pre-condition: Member instance exists.
   * 8. Post-condition: If true, a new BorrowRecord is added to borrowRecords with correct dates, and the book becomes unavailable to others. If false, borrowRecords and library state remain unchanged.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model todayDataType="edu.municipalLibrary.Date"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean borrowBook(final String bookTitle, final Date today)
  {
    Library library = getLibrary();
    if (library == null) {
      return false;
    }
    
    Document targetDoc = null;
    for (Document doc : library.getDocuments()) {
      if (doc.getTitle() != null && doc.getTitle().equals(bookTitle)) {
        targetDoc = doc;
        break;
      }
    }
    
    if (targetDoc == null || !(targetDoc instanceof Book)) {
      return false;
    }
    
    Book targetBook = (Book) targetDoc;
    for (Member member : library.getMembers()) {
      for (BorrowRecord record : member.getBorrowRecords()) {
        if (record.getBook() == targetBook) {
          return false;
        }
      }
    }
    
    if (getBorrowRecords().size() >= 3) {
      return false;
    }
    
    Calendar calendar = Calendar.getInstance();
    calendar.setTime(today);
    calendar.add(Calendar.DATE, 7);
    Date dueDate = calendar.getTime();
    
    BorrowRecord newRecord = new BorrowRecord();
    newRecord.setBorrowingDate(today);
    newRecord.setReturnDue(dueDate);
    newRecord.setBook(targetBook);
    
    getBorrowRecords().add(newRecord);
    return true;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * 1. Summary: Processes the return of a borrowed book, verifying ownership and compliance with overdue restrictions.
   * 2. Algorithm: 
   *    targetRecord = FIND record IN borrowRecords WHERE record.book.title EQUALS bookTitle
   *    IF targetRecord IS NULL THEN RETURN FALSE
   *    IF targetRecord.isOverdue(today) THEN RETURN FALSE
   *    borrowRecords.remove(targetRecord)
   *    RETURN TRUE
   * 3. Related Features: borrowRecords
   * 4. Related Classes: BorrowRecord, Book, Document
   * 5. Input: bookTitle (String): Title of the book being returned. today (Date): Current date. Format: "yyyy-MM-dd".
   * 6. Output: boolean: True if the return is accepted and the record is cleared, false otherwise.
   * 7. Pre-condition: Member instance exists.
   * 8. Post-condition: If true, the corresponding BorrowRecord is removed from borrowRecords, freeing the book for future loans. If false (book not held or overdue), borrowRecords remains completely unchanged.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model todayDataType="edu.municipalLibrary.Date"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean returnBook(final String bookTitle, final Date today)
  {
    BorrowRecord targetRecord = null;
    for (BorrowRecord record : getBorrowRecords()) {
      if (record.getBook() != null && record.getBook().getTitle() != null && record.getBook().getTitle().equals(bookTitle)) {
        targetRecord = record;
        break;
      }
    }
    
    if (targetRecord == null) {
      return false;
    }
    
    if (targetRecord.isOverdue(today)) {
      return false;
    }
    
    getBorrowRecords().remove(targetRecord);
    return true;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * 1. Summary: Requests an extension for the return due date of a specific borrowed book.
   * 2. Algorithm: 
   *    targetRecord = FIND record IN borrowRecords WHERE record.book.title EQUALS bookTitle
   *    IF targetRecord IS NULL THEN RETURN null (or handle as per error policy)
   *    RETURN targetRecord.extendDueDate(today)
   * 3. Related Features: borrowRecords
   * 4. Related Classes: BorrowRecord, Book, Document
   * 5. Input: bookTitle (String): Title of the borrowed book whose due date is to be extended. today (Date): Current date. Format: "yyyy-MM-dd".
   * 6. Output: Date: The resulting due date after evaluation. Format: "yyyy-MM-dd".
   * 7. Pre-condition: Member instance exists.
   * 8. Post-condition: Delegates temporal validation and date adjustment to BorrowRecord::extendDueDate. If the request window is valid, due date advances by 7 days; otherwise, it remains unchanged. Returns the final effective due date.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model dataType="edu.municipalLibrary.Date" todayDataType="edu.municipalLibrary.Date"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public Date extendReturnDueDate(final String bookTitle, final Date today)
  {
    BorrowRecord targetRecord = null;
    for (BorrowRecord record : getBorrowRecords()) {
      if (record.getBook() != null && record.getBook().getTitle() != null && record.getBook().getTitle().equals(bookTitle)) {
        targetRecord = record;
        break;
      }
    }
    
    if (targetRecord == null) {
      return null;
    }
    
    return targetRecord.extendDueDate(today);
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * 1. Summary: Retrieves a collection of titles for all books currently borrowed by the member.
   * 2. Algorithm: 
   *    titles = NEW List<String>()
   *    FOR EACH record IN borrowRecords DO
   *        titles.add(record.book.title)
   *    END FOR
   *    RETURN titles
   * 3. Related Features: borrowRecords
   * 4. Related Classes: BorrowRecord, Book, Document
   * 5. Input: None.
   * 6. Output: List<String>: A list containing the titles of all actively borrowed books. Returns an empty list if borrowRecords is empty.
   * 7. Pre-condition: Member instance exists.
   * 8. Post-condition: Returns a snapshot of current borrowed titles. No system state or borrowRecords are modified.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public EList<String> listBorrowedBookTitles()
  {
    EList<String> titles = new BasicEList<String>();
    for (BorrowRecord record : getBorrowRecords()) {
      if (record.getBook() != null) {
        titles.add(record.getBook().getTitle());
      }
    }
    return titles;
  }

```java
  protected static final Date RETURN_DUE_EDEFAULT = null;

  protected Date returnDue = RETURN_DUE_EDEFAULT;

  protected Book book;
```

  { ... /* omitted */ }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * 1. Summary: Extends the return due date of the borrow record by seven days if the extension request falls within the valid temporal window.
   * 2. Algorithm: 
   *    IF today >= borrowingDate AND today < returnDue THEN
   *        returnDue = returnDue + 7 days
   *    ENDIF
   *    RETURN returnDue
   * 3. Related Features: borrowingDate, returnDue
   * 4. Related Classes: None
   * 5. Input: today (Date): The date when the extension is requested. Format: "yyyy-MM-dd".
   * 6. Output: Date: The updated due date if conditions are met, otherwise the original due date. Format: "yyyy-MM-dd".
   * 7. Pre-condition: The borrow record is active and has not been returned or cancelled.
   * 8. Post-condition: If valid, returnDue is advanced by exactly 7 days. The method returns the final due date. If invalid, returnDue remains unchanged and the original date is returned.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model dataType="edu.municipalLibrary.Date" todayDataType="edu.municipalLibrary.Date"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public Date extendDueDate(final Date today)
  {
    Date borrowingDate = getBorrowingDate();
    Date currentReturnDue = getReturnDue();
    if (borrowingDate != null && currentReturnDue != null && today != null) {
        if (!today.before(borrowingDate) && today.before(currentReturnDue)) {
            Calendar cal = Calendar.getInstance();
            cal.setTime(currentReturnDue);
            cal.add(Calendar.DAY_OF_YEAR, 7);
            setReturnDue(cal.getTime());
        }
    }
    return getReturnDue();
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * 1. Summary: Determines whether the borrow record has exceeded its allowed borrowing period.
   * 2. Algorithm: RETURN today > returnDue
   * 3. Related Features: returnDue
   * 4. Related Classes: None
   * 5. Input: today (Date): The current date to evaluate against the due date. Format: "yyyy-MM-dd".
   * 6. Output: boolean: True if today is strictly after returnDue, false otherwise.
   * 7. Pre-condition: The borrow record exists and is active.
   * 8. Post-condition: No state modifications occur. The method returns a boolean reflecting the overdue status.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model todayDataType="edu.municipalLibrary.Date"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean isOverdue(final Date today)
  {
    return today != null && getReturnDue() != null && today.after(getReturnDue());
  }

# Task
You will be given a **Java Class** that is originally generated by EMF code generator with `suppressInterfaces=true` and later is completed by LLMs. Your task is to fix the **Compilation Errors** by taking additional contextual information into account.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information
  1.1 Consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.2 Locate these files in your project.
  1.3 Generate the class summarization.
2. Read the error messages carefully.
3. Do your best to fix the errors locally without introducing new types, fields, and methods.
4. You should not remove faulty methods to eliminate compilation errors.
5. Do not change parts without errors.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

```java
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * 1. Summary: Registers a new member in the library system after validating inputs and checking for naming conflicts.
   * 2. Algorithm: 
   *    IF firstName IS NULL OR EMPTY OR WHITESPACE_ONLY OR surname IS NULL OR EMPTY OR WHITESPACE_ONLY THEN RETURN FALSE
   *    fullName = firstName + " " + surname
   *    FOR EACH m IN members DO
   *        IF m.firstName + " " + m.surname EQUALS fullName THEN RETURN FALSE
   *    END FOR
   *    newMember = NEW Member(firstName, surname)
   *    newMember.library = THIS
   *    members.add(newMember)
   *    RETURN TRUE
   * 3. Related Features: members
   * 4. Related Classes: Member
   * 5. Input: firstName (String), surname (String). Both must not be null, empty, or whitespace-only.
   * 6. Output: boolean: True if registration succeeds, false if validation fails or a duplicate full name exists.
   * 7. Pre-condition: Library instance exists and is ready to accept new members.
   * 8. Post-condition: If true, a new Member with an empty borrowRecords list is created and added to members. If false, the library's member list remains unchanged.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean registerMember(final String firstName, final String surname)
  {
    if (firstName == null || firstName.trim().isEmpty() || surname == null || surname.trim().isEmpty()) {
      return false;
    }
    String fullName = firstName + " " + surname;
    for (Member m : getMembers()) {
      if ((m.getFirstName() + " " + m.getSurname()).equals(fullName)) {
        return false;
      }
    }
    Member newMember = new Member(firstName, surname);
    newMember.setLibrary(this);
    getMembers().add(newMember);
    return true;
  }
```

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.

  1.2 Locate these files in your project.

5. Do not change parts without errors.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

# Task
You will be given a **Requirement** and a **Class Diagram** in PlantUML. Your task is to annotate every operation listed in **Operations** with a specification.

An operation specification must describe the following sections about the operation:
1. Summary: the summary of functionality;
2. Algorithm: algorithm or pseudo code;
3. Related Features: a list of structural or behavior features within this class that shall be accessed;
4. Related Classes: a list of classes and data types defined in **Class Diagram** that shall be accessed, in the format of `Related Classes: ClsA, CLsB, ...` or `Related Classes: None`;
5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
7. Pre-condition: the condition that the system and the input must satisfy before the operation is executed;
8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

Finish this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Take the principle of modularization into account.
2. For each operation, determine its functional boundary based on the given requirement. Ensure high cohesion and low coupling.
3. If there is any unspecified details, such as undefined constants, rates, and ratios, then you must infer them.
4. Make sure that all requirements can be covered.

# Requirement
```text
We want to automate the management of a small municipal library. The library has a name and manages a collection of documents and a set of registered members. Members have a first name and a surname. Documents are uniquely identified by their title. Every document has a title. A volume is a kind of document and also has an author. A book is a kind of volume and also has a bookId that identifies the physical or catalogued copy. A dictionary is a kind of volume and also has a dictionaryId that identifies the dictionary entry in the library catalog. A comic is also a kind of volume and has a recipient name. A journal is a kind of document and has a publication date. Only books can be borrowed.
A registered member can borrow or return a book, and the system must always be able to list the titles currently borrowed by that member. A member can hold at most three borrowed books at the same time. When a borrowing succeeds, the system creates a borrow record containing the borrowing date, the borrowed book, and a return due date exactly seven days later. A borrowed book can be extended on request when the request is made before the current due date. A return is accepted only when the member currently holds that book and the book is not overdue. The system records dates in the format "yyyy-MM-dd".
Functional requirement:
1. Register a new member. The library registers a new member from a first name and a surname. The first name and surname must not be null, empty, or whitespace-only. The library rejects a registration when another member already has exactly the same full name. A successful registration creates the member with no borrowed books and returns true. Any rejected registration returns false.

2. Borrow a book and create a record in the system. A member borrows a document by title. The library accepts the request only when the title exists, the document is a book, the book is not already borrowed by any member, and the member currently holds fewer than three books. A successful borrowing creates a borrow record with today's date as the borrowing date and a due date exactly seven days later, then returns true. Any rejected borrowing returns false.

3. Extend a borrowed record's return due date. A member may extend the due date of one of their own borrowed books by title. When today is on or after the borrowing date and strictly before the current due date, the library moves the due date forward by seven days and returns the new due date. Otherwise, the due date remains unchanged and the original due date is returned.

4. Return a book. A member returns a borrowed book by title. If the member does not currently hold that title, the operation returns false. If the borrowed book is overdue, the return is rejected, the borrow record remains active, and the operation returns false. Otherwise the borrow record is removed and the operation returns true.

5. List all borrowed book titles for a member. A member can list the titles of all currently borrowed books. If the member has no active borrowed books, the operation returns an empty list.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title municipalLibrary Model

class Book {
    bookId : String
}
class BorrowRecord {
    borrowingDate : Date
    returnDue : Date
    book : Book
    extendDueDate(today : Date) : Date
    isOverdue(today : Date) : boolean
}
class Comic {
    recipientName : String
}
class Dictionary {
    dictionaryId : String
}
abstract class Document {
    title : String
}
class Journal {
    publicationDate : Date
}
class Library {
    name : String
    documents : List<Document>
    members : List<Member>
    addDocument(doc : Document) : void
    registerMember(firstName : String, surname : String) : boolean
}
class Member {
    firstName : String
    surname : String
    borrowRecords : List<BorrowRecord>
    library : Library
    borrowBook(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : boolean
    extendReturnDueDate(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : Date
    listBorrowedBookTitles() : List<String>
    returnBook(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : boolean
}
abstract class Volume {
    author : String
}

Book --|> Volume
BorrowRecord --> Book : book
Comic --|> Volume
Dictionary --|> Volume
Journal --|> Document
Library *-- "*" Document : documents
Library *-- "*" Member : members  'opposite: library
Member *-- "*" BorrowRecord : borrowRecords
Member --> Library : library  'opposite: members
Volume --|> Document
@enduml
```
