You will be given a **Requirement** and a **Class Diagram** in PlantUML. Your task is to annotate every operation listed in **Operations** with a specification.

An operation specification must describe the following sections about the operation:
1. Summary: the summary of functionality;
2. Algorithm: algorithm or pseudo code;
3. Related Features: a list of structural or behavior features within this class that shall be accessed;
4. Related Classes: a list of classes and data types defined in **Class Diagram** that shall be accessed, in the format of `Related Classes: ClsA, CLsB, ...` or `Related Classes: None`;
5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
7. Pre-condition: the condition that the system and the input must satisfy before the operation is executed;
8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

Finish this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Take the principle of modularization into account.
2. For each operation, determine its functional boundary based on the given requirement. Ensure high cohesion and low coupling.
3. If there is any unspecified details, such as undefined constants, rates, and ratios, then you must infer them.
4. Make sure that all requirements can be covered.

# Requirement
```text
We want to automate the management of a small municipal library. The library has a name and manages a collection of documents and a set of registered members. Members have a first name and a surname. Documents are uniquely identified by their title. Every document has a title. A volume is a kind of document and also has an author. A book is a kind of volume and also has a bookId that identifies the physical or catalogued copy. A dictionary is a kind of volume and also has a dictionaryId that identifies the dictionary entry in the library catalog. A comic is also a kind of volume and has a recipient name. A journal is a kind of document and has a publication date. Only books can be borrowed.
A registered member can borrow or return a book, and the system must always be able to list the titles currently borrowed by that member. A member can hold at most three borrowed books at the same time. When a borrowing succeeds, the system creates a borrow record containing the borrowing date, the borrowed book, and a return due date exactly seven days later. A borrowed book can be extended on request when the request is made before the current due date. A return is accepted only when the member currently holds that book and the book is not overdue. The system records dates in the format "yyyy-MM-dd".
Functional requirement:
1. Register a new member. The library registers a new member from a first name and a surname. The first name and surname must not be null, empty, or whitespace-only. The library rejects a registration when another member already has exactly the same full name. A successful registration creates the member with no borrowed books and returns true. Any rejected registration returns false.

2. Borrow a book and create a record in the system. A member borrows a document by title. The library accepts the request only when the title exists, the document is a book, the book is not already borrowed by any member, and the member currently holds fewer than three books. A successful borrowing creates a borrow record with today's date as the borrowing date and a due date exactly seven days later, then returns true. Any rejected borrowing returns false.

3. Extend a borrowed record's return due date. A member may extend the due date of one of their own borrowed books by title. When today is on or after the borrowing date and strictly before the current due date, the library moves the due date forward by seven days and returns the new due date. Otherwise, the due date remains unchanged and the original due date is returned.

4. Return a book. A member returns a borrowed book by title. If the member does not currently hold that title, the operation returns false. If the borrowed book is overdue, the return is rejected, the borrow record remains active, and the operation returns false. Otherwise the borrow record is removed and the operation returns true.

5. List all borrowed book titles for a member. A member can list the titles of all currently borrowed books. If the member has no active borrowed books, the operation returns an empty list.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title municipalLibrary Model

class Book {
    bookId : String
}
class BorrowRecord {
    borrowingDate : Date
    returnDue : Date
    book : Book
    extendDueDate(today : Date) : Date
    isOverdue(today : Date) : boolean
}
class Comic {
    recipientName : String
}
class Dictionary {
    dictionaryId : String
}
abstract class Document {
    title : String
}
class Journal {
    publicationDate : Date
}
class Library {
    name : String
    documents : List<Document>
    members : List<Member>
    addDocument(doc : Document) : void
    registerMember(firstName : String, surname : String) : boolean
}
class Member {
    firstName : String
    surname : String
    borrowRecords : List<BorrowRecord>
    library : Library
    borrowBook(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : boolean
    extendReturnDueDate(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : Date
    listBorrowedBookTitles() : List<String>
    returnBook(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : boolean
}
abstract class Volume {
    author : String
}

Book --|> Volume
BorrowRecord --> Book : book
Comic --|> Volume
Dictionary --|> Volume
Journal --|> Document
Library *-- "*" Document : documents
Library *-- "*" Member : members  'opposite: library
Member *-- "*" BorrowRecord : borrowRecords
Member --> Library : library  'opposite: members
Volume --|> Document
@enduml
```

# Operation Specifications
BorrowRecord::isOverdue
1. Summary: Determines whether the borrowed book has exceeded its return due date relative to the provided current date.
2. Algorithm: 
   - Return today > returnDue.
3. Related Features: returnDue
4. Related Classes: Date
5. Input: today (Current system date, format "yyyy-MM-dd")
6. Output: boolean (true if overdue, false otherwise)
7. Pre-condition: The BorrowRecord instance exists and today is a valid date.
8. Post-condition: Returns true if and only if today is strictly greater than returnDue. The internal state of the record remains unchanged.

BorrowRecord::extendDueDate
1. Summary: Extends the return due date of the borrow record by seven days if the request is made within the valid window (from borrowing date up to, but not including, the current due date).
2. Algorithm: 
   - If today >= borrowingDate AND today < returnDue:
       - Calculate newDue = returnDue + 7 days.
       - Update returnDue = newDue.
   - Return returnDue.
3. Related Features: borrowingDate, returnDue
4. Related Classes: Date
5. Input: today (Current system date, format "yyyy-MM-dd")
6. Output: Date (The resulting due date after potential extension, format "yyyy-MM-dd")
7. Pre-condition: The BorrowRecord instance exists and today is a valid date.
8. Post-condition: If the condition in step 2 is met, returnDue is advanced by exactly seven days and matches the returned value. Otherwise, returnDue remains unchanged and matches the returned value.

# Requirement
```text
We want to automate the management of a small municipal library. The library has a name and manages a collection of documents and a set of registered members. Members have a first name and a surname. Documents are uniquely identified by their title. Every document has a title. A volume is a kind of document and also has an author. A book is a kind of volume and also has a bookId that identifies the physical or catalogued copy. A dictionary is a kind of volume and also has a dictionaryId that identifies the dictionary entry in the library catalog. A comic is also a kind of volume and has a recipient name. A journal is a kind of document and has a publication date. Only books can be borrowed.
A registered member can borrow or return a book, and the system must always be able to list the titles currently borrowed by that member. A member can hold at most three borrowed books at the same time. When a borrowing succeeds, the system creates a borrow record containing the borrowing date, the borrowed book, and a return due date exactly seven days later. A borrowed book can be extended on request when the request is made before the current due date. A return is accepted only when the member currently holds that book and the book is not overdue. The system records dates in the format "yyyy-MM-dd".
Functional requirement:
1. Register a new member. The library registers a new member from a first name and a surname. The first name and surname must not be null, empty, or whitespace-only. The library rejects a registration when another member already has exactly the same full name. A successful registration creates the member with no borrowed books and returns true. Any rejected registration returns false.

2. Borrow a book and create a record in the system. A member borrows a document by title. The library accepts the request only when the title exists, the document is a book, the book is not already borrowed by any member, and the member currently holds fewer than three books. A successful borrowing creates a borrow record with today's date as the borrowing date and a due date exactly seven days later, then returns true. Any rejected borrowing returns false.

3. Extend a borrowed record's return due date. A member may extend the due date of one of their own borrowed books by title. When today is on or after the borrowing date and strictly before the current due date, the library moves the due date forward by seven days and returns the new due date. Otherwise, the due date remains unchanged and the original due date is returned.

4. Return a book. A member returns a borrowed book by title. If the member does not currently hold that title, the operation returns false. If the borrowed book is overdue, the return is rejected, the borrow record remains active, and the operation returns false. Otherwise the borrow record is removed and the operation returns true.

5. List all borrowed book titles for a member. A member can list the titles of all currently borrowed books. If the member has no active borrowed books, the operation returns an empty list.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title municipalLibrary Model

class Book {
    bookId : String
}
class BorrowRecord {
    borrowingDate : Date
    returnDue : Date
    book : Book
    extendDueDate(today : Date) : Date
    isOverdue(today : Date) : boolean
}
class Comic {
    recipientName : String
}
class Dictionary {
    dictionaryId : String
}
abstract class Document {
    title : String
}
class Journal {
    publicationDate : Date
}
class Library {
    name : String
    documents : List<Document>
    members : List<Member>
    addDocument(doc : Document) : void
    registerMember(firstName : String, surname : String) : boolean
}
class Member {
    firstName : String
    surname : String
    borrowRecords : List<BorrowRecord>
    library : Library
    borrowBook(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : boolean
    extendReturnDueDate(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : Date
    listBorrowedBookTitles() : List<String>
    returnBook(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : boolean
}
abstract class Volume {
    author : String
}

Book --|> Volume
BorrowRecord --> Book : book
Comic --|> Volume
Dictionary --|> Volume
Journal --|> Document
Library *-- "*" Document : documents
Library *-- "*" Member : members  'opposite: library
Member *-- "*" BorrowRecord : borrowRecords
Member --> Library : library  'opposite: members
Volume --|> Document
@enduml
```

# Operation Specifications
BorrowRecord::isOverdue
1. Summary: Determines whether the borrowed book has exceeded its return due date relative to the provided current date.
2. Algorithm: 
   - Return today > returnDue.
3. Related Features: returnDue
4. Related Classes: Date
5. Input: today (Current system date, format "yyyy-MM-dd")
6. Output: boolean (true if overdue, false otherwise)
7. Pre-condition: The BorrowRecord instance exists and today is a valid date.
8. Post-condition: Returns true if and only if today is strictly greater than returnDue. The internal state of the record remains unchanged.

BorrowRecord::extendDueDate
1. Summary: Extends the return due date of the borrow record by seven days if the request is made within the valid window (from borrowing date up to, but not including, the current due date).
2. Algorithm: 
   - If today >= borrowingDate AND today < returnDue:
       - Calculate newDue = returnDue + 7 days.
       - Update returnDue = newDue.
   - Return returnDue.
3. Related Features: borrowingDate, returnDue
4. Related Classes: Date
5. Input: today (Current system date, format "yyyy-MM-dd")
6. Output: Date (The resulting due date after potential extension, format "yyyy-MM-dd")
7. Pre-condition: The BorrowRecord instance exists and today is a valid date.
8. Post-condition: If the condition in step 2 is met, returnDue is advanced by exactly seven days and matches the returned value. Otherwise, returnDue remains unchanged and matches the returned value.

# Operation Specifications
Library::registerMember
1. Summary: Registers a new library member after validating input fields and verifying name uniqueness across existing members.
2. Algorithm: 
   - Validate firstName and surname are not null, empty, or whitespace-only. If invalid, return false.
   - Iterate through this.members to check if any existing member's full name equals firstName + " " + surname. If found, return false.
   - Create a new Member instance with the given names, initialize borrowRecords as an empty list, and set the member's library reference to this.
   - Add the new member to this.members.
   - Return true.
3. Related Features: name, members
4. Related Classes: Member, String, List
5. Input: firstName (String, non-null/non-whitespace), surname (String, non-null/non-whitespace)
6. Output: boolean (true if successfully registered, false if validation fails or name already exists)
7. Pre-condition: firstName and surname are provided strings.
8. Post-condition: If true, a new Member with the specified names and an empty borrowRecords list is added to members. If false, the members list remains unchanged.

Library::addDocument
1. Summary: Adds a new document to the library's collection of managed documents.
2. Algorithm: 
   - Verify doc is not null.
   - Append doc to this.documents.
3. Related Features: name, documents
4. Related Classes: Document
5. Input: doc (A Document object to be added; must have a non-null, non-empty title)
6. Output: void
7. Pre-condition: doc is not null and doc.title is valid.
8. Post-condition: this.documents contains doc. The library's catalog size increases by one. (Title uniqueness is enforced at the document creation level per domain rules.)

5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
7. Pre-condition: the condition that the system and the input must satisfy before the operation is executed;
8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

Finish this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Read the requirement and the class diagram.
2. For each operation specification, determine whether it is complete and consistent with the requirement.
3. If there is any error, correct the erroneous specification.
4. Follow the specification format. Output changed specifications only; OMIT unchanged specifications.

# Requirement
```text
We want to automate the management of a small municipal library. The library has a name and manages a collection of documents and a set of registered members. Members have a first name and a surname. Documents are uniquely identified by their title. Every document has a title. A volume is a kind of document and also has an author. A book is a kind of volume and also has a bookId that identifies the physical or catalogued copy. A dictionary is a kind of volume and also has a dictionaryId that identifies the dictionary entry in the library catalog. A comic is also a kind of volume and has a recipient name. A journal is a kind of document and has a publication date. Only books can be borrowed.
A registered member can borrow or return a book, and the system must always be able to list the titles currently borrowed by that member. A member can hold at most three borrowed books at the same time. When a borrowing succeeds, the system creates a borrow record containing the borrowing date, the borrowed book, and a return due date exactly seven days later. A borrowed book can be extended on request when the request is made before the current due date. A return is accepted only when the member currently holds that book and the book is not overdue. The system records dates in the format "yyyy-MM-dd".
Functional requirement:
1. Register a new member. The library registers a new member from a first name and a surname. The first name and surname must not be null, empty, or whitespace-only. The library rejects a registration when another member already has exactly the same full name. A successful registration creates the member with no borrowed books and returns true. Any rejected registration returns false.

2. Borrow a book and create a record in the system. A member borrows a document by title. The library accepts the request only when the title exists, the document is a book, the book is not already borrowed by any member, and the member currently holds fewer than three books. A successful borrowing creates a borrow record with today's date as the borrowing date and a due date exactly seven days later, then returns true. Any rejected borrowing returns false.

3. Extend a borrowed record's return due date. A member may extend the due date of one of their own borrowed books by title. When today is on or after the borrowing date and strictly before the current due date, the library moves the due date forward by seven days and returns the new due date. Otherwise, the due date remains unchanged and the original due date is returned.

4. Return a book. A member returns a borrowed book by title. If the member does not currently hold that title, the operation returns false. If the borrowed book is overdue, the return is rejected, the borrow record remains active, and the operation returns false. Otherwise the borrow record is removed and the operation returns true.

5. List all borrowed book titles for a member. A member can list the titles of all currently borrowed books. If the member has no active borrowed books, the operation returns an empty list.
```

    surname : String

    extendReturnDueDate(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : Date

Journal --|> Document

# Operation Specifications
Member::listBorrowedBookTitles
1. Summary: Retrieves a list of titles for all books currently borrowed by this member.
2. Algorithm: 
   - Initialize an empty list resultTitles.
   - For each record in this.borrowRecords:
       - Append record.book.title to resultTitles.
   - Return resultTitles.
3. Related Features: borrowRecords
4. Related Classes: BorrowRecord, List, String
5. Input: None
6. Output: List<String> (Collection of book titles currently borrowed; empty list if none)
7. Pre-condition: Member exists.
8. Post-condition: Returns a list containing exactly the titles of all books associated with active borrow records. Order corresponds to insertion order in borrowRecords.

Member::returnBook
1. Summary: Processes the return of a borrowed book, validating ownership and overdue status before removing the borrow record.
2. Algorithm: 
   - Search this.borrowRecords for a BorrowRecord where record.book.title == bookTitle. If not found, return false.
   - If record.isOverdue(today) returns true, return false.
   - Remove the matched record from this.borrowRecords.
   - Return true.
3. Related Features: borrowRecords
4. Related Classes: BorrowRecord, Date, List, String
5. Input: bookTitle (String), today (Date, format "yyyy-MM-dd")
6. Output: boolean (true if return is accepted, false otherwise)
7. Pre-condition: Member exists. bookTitle corresponds to a book previously borrowed by this member. today is a valid date.
8. Post-condition: If true, the corresponding BorrowRecord is removed from borrowRecords. If false, borrowRecords remains unchanged.

Member::borrowBook
1. Summary: Attempts to borrow a specific book by title, creating a borrow record if all business rules are satisfied.
2. Algorithm: 
   - If this.borrowRecords.size() >= 3, return false.
   - Search this.library.documents for a Document whose title matches bookTitle. If not found, return false.
   - If the found document is not an instance of Book, return false.
   - Check if this Book is already borrowed by iterating through all members' borrowRecords in this.library.members. If any record's book matches the target book, return false.
   - Create a new BorrowRecord with borrowingDate = today, returnDue = today + 7 days, and book = target book.
   - Add the new record to this.borrowRecords.
   - Return true.
3. Related Features: firstName, surname, borrowRecords, library
4. Related Classes: Book, Document, BorrowRecord, Date, List, Library
5. Input: bookTitle (String, exact title match required), today (Date, format "yyyy-MM-dd")
6. Output: boolean (true if borrowing succeeds, false otherwise)
7. Pre-condition: Member exists. bookTitle is a valid string. today is a valid date.
8. Post-condition: If true, a new BorrowRecord is added to borrowRecords with correct dates and book reference. State reflects the new loan. If false, state remains unchanged.

Member::extendReturnDueDate
1. Summary: Extends the due date of a specific borrowed book by seven days if the request falls within the permissible time window.
2. Algorithm: 
   - Search this.borrowRecords for a BorrowRecord where record.book.title == bookTitle. If not found, return null.
   - Invoke record.extendDueDate(today).
   - Return the result from the previous step.
3. Related Features: borrowRecords
4. Related Classes: BorrowRecord, Date, List, String
5. Input: bookTitle (String), today (Date, format "yyyy-MM-dd")
6. Output: Date (New due date if extended, original due date if conditions fail, or null if book not in member's records)
7. Pre-condition: Member has at least one borrow record. today is a valid date.
8. Post-condition: If record exists and today satisfies borrowingDate <= today < returnDue, returnDue is advanced by 7 days. Returned value matches the final returnDue. If conditions fail, returnDue is unchanged. Returned value matches returnDue.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

   * 1. Summary: Attempts to borrow a specific book by title, creating a borrow record if all business rules are satisfied.
   * 2. Algorithm: 
   *    - If this.borrowRecords.size() >= 3, return false.
   *    - Search this.library.documents for a Document whose title matches bookTitle. If not found, return false.
   *    - If the found document is not an instance of Book, return false.
   *    - Check if this Book is already borrowed by iterating through all members' borrowRecords in this.library.members. If any record's book matches the target book, return false.
   *    - Create a new BorrowRecord with borrowingDate = today, returnDue = today + 7 days, and book = target book.
   *    - Add the new record to this.borrowRecords.
   *    - Return true.
   * 3. Related Features: borrowRecords, library
   * 4. Related Classes: Book, Document, BorrowRecord, Library
   * 5. Input: bookTitle (String, exact title match required), today (Date, format "yyyy-MM-dd")
   * 6. Output: boolean (true if borrowing succeeds, false otherwise)
   * 7. Pre-condition: Member exists. bookTitle is a valid string. today is a valid date.
   * 8. Post-condition: If true, a new BorrowRecord is added to borrowRecords with correct dates and book reference. State reflects the new loan. If false, state remains unchanged.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model todayDataType="edu.municipalLibrary.Date"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean borrowBook(final String bookTitle, final Date today)
  {
    if (this.getBorrowRecords().size() >= 3) {
      return false;
    }
    
    Library library = this.getLibrary();
    if (library == null) {
      return false;
    }
    
    Document foundDoc = null;
    for (Document doc : library.getDocuments()) {
      if (doc.getTitle() != null && doc.getTitle().equals(bookTitle)) {
        foundDoc = doc;
        break;
      }
    }
    
    if (foundDoc == null || !(foundDoc instanceof Book)) {
      return false;
    }
    
    Book targetBook = (Book) foundDoc;
    
    for (Member member : library.getMembers()) {
      for (BorrowRecord record : member.getBorrowRecords()) {
        if (record.getBook() == targetBook) {
          return false;
        }
      }
    }
    
    BorrowRecord newRecord = MunicipalLibraryFactory.eINSTANCE.createBorrowRecord();
    newRecord.setBorrowingDate(today);
    
    Calendar cal = Calendar.getInstance();
    cal.setTime(today);
    cal.add(Calendar.DATE, 7);
    newRecord.setReturnDue(cal.getTime());
    
    newRecord.setBook(targetBook);
    this.getBorrowRecords().add(newRecord);
    
    return true;
  }

  public boolean returnBook(final String bookTitle, final Date today)
  {
    BorrowRecord matchedRecord = null;
    for (BorrowRecord record : this.getBorrowRecords()) {
      if (record.getBook() != null && record.getBook().getTitle() != null && record.getBook().getTitle().equals(bookTitle)) {
        matchedRecord = record;
        break;
      }
    }
    
    if (matchedRecord == null) {
      return false;
    }
    
    if (matchedRecord.isOverdue(today)) {
      return false;
    }
    
    this.getBorrowRecords().remove(matchedRecord);
    return true;
  }

  public Date extendReturnDueDate(final String bookTitle, final Date today)

    BorrowRecord matchedRecord = null;
    for (BorrowRecord record : this.getBorrowRecords()) {
      if (record.getBook() != null && record.getBook().getTitle() != null && record.getBook().getTitle().equals(bookTitle)) {
        matchedRecord = record;

      }
    }
    
    if (matchedRecord == null) {
      return null;
    }
    
    if (!today.before(matchedRecord.getBorrowingDate()) && today.before(matchedRecord.getReturnDue())) {

      return matchedRecord.getReturnDue();
    } else {

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.

  1.2 Locate these files in your project.

2. Read the error messages carefully.

5. Do not change parts without errors.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.
